Null === TheGestalt

TheGestalt === {Gestalt[[N]],Conjugate[Gestalt[[N]]]}            N Pairs    N = 'Count Phase' = number of reflections     N = {1,2,3,..,N,N+1}

Pair production: Gestalt and Conjugate of Gestalt --- The pair is the conceptional, neither exist without the other. the Conjugate is the 'shaddow' of 'something' on the Null plane. Something = not Null  the shaddow the 'hole' (think simiconductor doping) left in the Null by 'Something' 

Conjugent = Reflection = Rotation = Spin = Toruq = vector ('away', negitive with count 1, no spacal but  ALL dimitional complex plain directions of away. Need to sho that perojects into the reals as a vector with no specal demention just '-' (Away from observer, where obsver was at start of measurment [minimum measrment one reflection]) the time diminiont; tork of the complex.sponior Plankette  See MacGreagoe Power of Alpha ch 5 up and his zeons.

Planko very by phase, only one phase per Planko, phases outhorgnormal --- Pauli exclusing prisiple on phase phase = count Reflections from arbertary start point
